# YAML value results — YV-R01

Same-author execution and audit; independent CDC pending. All 259 normal variants ran twice in fresh roots (518 attempts). Three supervisor controls and five harness controls twice are separate. Tables classify observed outcomes; a complete transport/workflow status does not imply semantic or document fidelity.

Source, runtime, graph and scope are pinned in baseline-and-protocol.md. Every warning event is retained in ordered stdout and the detailed records below. Bound markers mean incomplete observations; no such marker is accepted in the small normal matrix. Y-13/Y-16 and gated Y-14 remain future work.
